Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.992 (Extension Principle)

Statement: Let the general_chemistry structure S satisfy the conditions of extension principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 729. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 903 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the extension principle condition on general_chemistry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 729. The convergence follows from the extension principle estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 903. QED.

Statement: The general_chemistry variety V defined by isomorphism class constraints admits a stratification into 498 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 548 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the perturbation bound principle to the general_chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^445 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 429-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
